# Derivation register

**Layer 4 companion artifact 13 · rev 13 · AssetOps Architect (Claude) · 17 Nov 2026 · restated 22 Nov 2026 (0K4m, D-0K4.31 to D-0K4.49)**

**0K4m restatement (22 Nov).** The owner's one-figure rule (21 Nov) retires the class "replay replay placeholder". The §3.1 rows that held one now name what replaced it: a v8 study figure read from `replay-studies.json` (K8 Kobo, M8 Mto; SIMULATED, MODELLED, with the study's assumptions), `unavailable("<record>")` ("Not available yet", naming the record that would fill it), `information` beside an option (the dawn margin, never a gate), or a world-view record held for 0K4f. The counts in §1 and the perturbation results are the 17 Nov scan's, unchanged. §7 is the 17 Nov sweep of the mock-up's code, and 0K4m unit 3 replaces the replay-placeholder labels it lists with the Simulated chip and the source fields.

This is the main deliverable of rev 13 (owner, twelfth message, rule 2). It lists every item in the pack that is **not derived** from the declared structure (`structure-specification.md`), wherever the item lives: manifest, evaluator, checker, mock-up code and generators, documents.

Each item has one of three dispositions:
- **(a) derived**, with how it is derived and a perturbation that moves it;
- **(b) a declared input**: a record, quote, replay study figure (or "Not available yet"), assumption, owner decision, policy declaration or structure declaration, with its flag;
- **(c) presentation only**: it affects no figure or choice.

**How the items were found.**
- **Manifest.** Every leaf (2,438) was perturbed alone in five worlds (base; Mto, Kasarani and Thika claims refused; Kobo battery covered). A leaf that moved any evaluated value or choice, or without which the evaluation could not run, is listed individually in §3.1. Every other leaf is listed individually in §3.2. The scan's test is "moves an evaluated value"; it does not see a leaf the screen renders straight from the manifest. The final review's (c)-scan found that 243 of the 477 numeric §3.2 leaves can change with no check failing (documented limit F3). 175 manifest leaves (`oracle`, `checks`, `sources`, `time_scales`, `chain_model`, `calibration_spikes` and metadata) were outside the scan; see L1 in §9.
- **Evaluator and checker.** Read rule by rule (§4, §5).
- **Mock-up and generators.** The builder's sweep (§7).
- **Documents.** The anchor scan (§6).
- **Items no perturbation can reveal.** Listed in §8.

## 2. Derived items (disposition a), each with a perturbation that moves it

Each item is computed under `structure-specification.md` from declared inputs. The evaluator and the independent model compute it separately, and the checker compares them. The perturbation IDs refer to the rev 13 harness (`scratchpad/perturb4.py`). Its results are in REVIEW-response.md "## Author response (rev 13)".

### 3.3 Empty states (`empty_states`, rev 16.13, 20 Nov)

Added after rev 13's scan (owner routing of 20 Nov, `owner/owner-placeholder-gaps-routing-2026-11-20.md`). None of these leaves moves a figure or a choice: the evaluator's figures do not read them, and `derived-values.json` is byte-identical before and after (sha256 `bfe2cbc5…813a`). They move `missing-records.json` only.

| Block | Leaves | Disposition |
|---|---|---|
| `empty_states.leaf_sources` | one row per `factor_map` leaf (31 mini-grid, 23 cold, 23 EV): family, kind, absent, slice, plain words where shown | (b) structure declaration (spec §3.1) |
| `empty_states.target_side` | the target line's kind, family, slice, words and the target assumption's label | (b) structure declaration; the label marks an **assumption** (spec §1, §3.1 rule 2) |
| `empty_states.conditions` | the record behind each MISSING condition (battery temperature, commissioning capacity test, 1,000-hour service) | (b) structure declaration |
| `empty_states.assumptions` | the record that would confirm a field marked "[assumption]" (Embakasi's tariff applicability) | (b) structure declaration |
| `empty_states.chain_nodes` | each `chain_model` stage and leak, with the nodes, potential entry, deferred stage or reconciled zero that carries it | (b) structure declaration |
| `empty_states.deferred_stages` | mini-grid usable capacity: readings, cause, what it blocks, queued slice `4c`, trigger | (b) policy declaration (Architect's call, decisions register call 23) |
| `empty_states.queued_slices` | `4c`: scope, lane, units, dependencies | (b) policy declaration; outside `plan_model.py` |
| `empty_states.record_words` | plain words for a record kind on screen | (c) presentation |
| `home_categories.potential.MINI_GRID[headroom].deferred` | replaces the free-text `shown` copy, which blamed a missing export | (b) structure declaration |

### 3.4 Reconciliation (`home_categories.reconciliation`, rev 16.14, 20 Nov)

Added for the UX audit's C2 (rulings D-RECON.1 to D-RECON.4). No leaf here moves a figure or a choice: the evaluator and the independent model do not read the block, and `derived-values.json` is byte-identical (H8 green). The block moves only `check_manifest.py` `reconciliation()` and, through the fixtures, the screens' words.

| Block | Leaves | Disposition |
|---|---|---|
| `home_categories.reconciliation.rule` | the rule in words | (c) presentation |

Added for OD-26 (rulings D-L4OPS.1 to D-L4OPS.8). No leaf here moves a figure or a choice: the evaluator, the independent model and the history generator do not read the block, it is outside `view_digest`, and `derived-values.json`, `missing-records.json` and `history.json` are byte-identical after `evaluate_structure.py` (H8 and H11 green; H10 re-based). ## 4. Evaluator code (`evaluate_structure.py`)

Every rule here is also a rule of `structure-specification.md`. The independent model implements it separately from that text, and the checker compares the two programs on every computed path, so a rule that drifts from the specification fails the check. Items marked † could not be revealed by perturbing an input; §7 lists them again.

| # | Where | Item | What it determines | Disposition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| E1 | `Env.ev`, `BIN`, `FUNCS`, `DATE_FUNCS` | the expression language: + − × ÷, unary −, min, max, ceil, floor, days_incl, days_between, nights_listed, `a \| b` fallback | how every declared expression is read | (b) structure declaration (spec §2.4) † |
| E2 | `Env.ev` `\|` | a leaf that cannot be resolved falls back to the declared alternative (often `0`); there is no default anywhere else | which records feed a leaf. A missing record silently gives the fallback value. | (b) structure declaration: each fallback is written in `factor_map` (register §3.1) † |
| E3 | `money()` | rounding half-even to 0.01 on output only (computation stays exact decimal) | every reported figure | (b) structure declaration (spec §2.4) † |
| E4 | `add_business_days` | business days = Monday to Friday; no holiday calendar | SLA restore dates; the huller's connection date | (b) structure declaration (spec §2.1); a holiday calendar is a documented limit † |
| E5 | `NOTIFIED` | claim states that mean "notice given": NOTIFIED, ACKNOWLEDGED, ACCEPTED, DENIED, REJECTED | the notice-deadline lapse | (b) structure declaration (spec §4.1; brief l.430 states) |
| E6 | `covered()` | covered = POTENTIALLY_COVERED and the claim is not DENIED or REJECTED | who bears each cost line; claimable cover; supplier restores | (b) structure declaration (spec §4.1) |
| E7 | `rights_nodes` | `unclaimable_cover` counts INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE rights only | the cover bound | (b) structure declaration (spec §4.1) |
| E8 | main, rights collection | a right's deadline already past at as_of with no notice given → LAPSED for every option, not counted as an option's loss | GT1 | (a) derived from the record's dates (spec §4.1); perturbation GT1 |
| E9 | `touches` / `deciders` | which options decide a right: those touching it by cost line, restore, void, set or notice, plus their decision's other options | the lapse scope (rev 12 M5) | (a) derived from the declared options and decisions; perturbations H1n, X1, GT1 |
| E10 | `run`, lapse rule | an un-notified right with a deadline lapses for a deciding option that files no notice by the deadline | MTO-INV-NOACT rejection | (a) derived (spec §4.1); perturbations X1, H1n |
| E11 | `run`, restore | `performed_under` + `if_not_covered`: a refused right switches the restore to the operator's own procurement | restore dates under a refusal (rev 12 L1) | (a) derived from rights and declared quotes; perturbations RA, RB, A10 |
| E12 | `run`, horizons | protect days capped at `horizon_end` + 1; value days capped at as_of + `horizon_months` − 1 day; "never restored" runs to each end | Protect effects and fuel money (rev 12 M2) | (a) derived from declared horizons; perturbations A05, A06 |
| E13 | `value_end` | horizon months are added as whole years (`horizon_months // 12`) | the value horizon end | (b) structure declaration; only whole years are supported, a documented limit † |
| E14 | `run`, params | parameters resolve in declared order; those reading `restore_date` resolve after the restore | the Kasarani spare cover | (b) structure declaration (spec §4.2) † |
| E15 | `run`, forward baseline | the leaves of `evaluation.forward_baseline[V]` are reset before an option's changes | forward options' Protect effects (rev 11 L5) | (b) structure declaration (manifest) |
| E16 | `run`, months | `months` defaults to 1 (the option's effect counted once over its horizon) | every restore-path value | (b) structure declaration (spec §5.4) † |
| E17 | `run`, value | value = Δ × months − (one-off − reference one-off) | every option value | (b) structure declaration (spec §5.4) |
| E18 | `run`, `money_changes` | valued in money iff Δ ≠ 0, not a comparator, not `is_reference` | what is valued vs shown on Protect | (b) structure declaration (spec §5.5) |
| E19 | `run`, crews | crew visits from the option or its row; never defaulted; unknown → the combination is infeasible | crew feasibility | (b) structure declaration (spec §5.7) |
| E20 | main, sides | the sides are plan, actual (merged with the October observations) and November to date | what "October" and "to date" mean | (b) structure declaration (spec §2.2) |
| E21 | main | the vertical `EV_DEPOT` is read as `EV_DEPOT_CAPTIVE` | which structure applies at Metro | (b) structure declaration (independent ambiguity A1) † |
| E22 | main, not decomposed | a side whose leaves cannot resolve is "not decomposed" with its stated contribution or total | Ruiru's plan | (b) structure declaration (spec §2.2) |
| E23 | `best`, candidates | protective: options meeting the hard bounds, else all of them (with the fallback); valued: the same over options valued in money, plus "none" if optional | the choice sets | (b) structure declaration (spec §6.1) |
| E24 | `best`, feasibility | budget (the case's budget switch, else the plan budget), crew limit, `requires_switch`, `requires_restore` | plan feasibility | (a) derived for dated dependencies (A01, A02, LT7, LS20); (b) budget and limits are owner decisions |
| E25 | `best`, rank | key (− total violation of fallback decisions, value, − spend) | which plan is chosen | (b) structure declaration (spec §6.3) |
| E26 | `best`, ties | the first maximal combination in enumeration order (declared decision order, "none" first) | ties only (none in the demonstration world) | (b) structure declaration (spec §6.3) † |
| E27 | `best`, trade-off | reported when another option of a protective decision has a smaller violation | the Kisiwa trade-off line | (a) derived; perturbation L2, B1500 |
| E28 | cases | the switch product in declared order; key = values joined with "/" (`True`/`False`) | case names | (b) structure declaration (spec §6.4) |
| E29 | sensitivities, downside | sensitivity values, switch-sensitivity values, drops; downside = minimum | the downside | (b) structure declaration (spec §6.4) |
| E30 | portfolio | sums when all sites are evaluated; otherwise stated totals and cost per departure required | portfolio headline | (b) structure declaration (spec §3) |
| E31 | O2 | Σ numerator / Σ denominator × scale, rounded to declared decimals (default 2; scale default 1) | O2 measures | (b) structure declaration (spec §3) † |
| E32 | owner-question variants | `set` / `remove` patches on a copy; value and spend change against the applied variant | Q15 figures | (a) derived; perturbation A08, Q15B |
| E33 | `rows` | an option's row is found by id in the site's decision, entitlement or entitlements options | `row.` reads | (b) structure declaration (spec §2.3) |
| E34 | status strings, labels ("LAPSED", "VOIDED", "least violation within the plan", "meets its hard bounds") | text only | (c) presentation |
| E35 | `missing_records()`, `leftmost()`, `ancestors()` (rev 16.13) | an entry per observed leaf no record fills, per coverage-ESTIMATED line (carried at target), per MISSING condition, per "[assumption]" field, per deferred stage, and per target side that is not decomposed (residual) or whose outcome-feeding leaves have no target line of their own (labelled assumption, only where actual and target differ); `blocks` breadth-first up the structure | `missing-records.json` only; no figure | (b) structure declaration (spec §3.1); checked by C13 |

## 7. Mock-up code and generators


**Scope and method.** This register lists every item in the mock-up's own code that is not read from DERIVED (`../derived-values.json`, embedded in `fixtures.js`) or from MANIFEST (`../demo-site-scenario-manifest.yaml`, embedded in `fixtures.js`). It covers the 15 files named in the request, in that order. Each file was read in full, line by line. For every constant, map, label table, rewrite rule, ordering, threshold, regex, typed fact sentence, evidence-class rule, disposition rule, route list, alias, allow-list entry, hard-coded option or site choice and screen-side calculation, the register records what it determines on screen (or in the checks and generated documents) and how it is justified under `../structure-specification.md` §1 (declared inputs) and §7 (conformance of screens). Typed figures were checked against the manifest and DERIVED by searching for the value. A typed figure that repeats a value already held at a manifest or DERIVED path is flagged "RESTATES <path>". A typed fact with no backing record or decision found is flagged "UNBACKED: typed narrative". "RESTATES-risk" marks code that reads a stated manifest copy where DERIVED holds the evaluated value. Line numbers are those of the files as of 17 Nov 2026 on branch `task/product-direction-layer4-design`. The v7.0 files that are re-based but not authored here (`screens.js`, `portfolio.js`, `outcomes.js`, CSS) are out of scope.

**Disposition legend.**

- **(a) derived**: computed from DERIVED or from the manifest, including screen-side arithmetic. The cell says from what. Screen-side derivations that the evaluator does not produce are marked as such.
- **(b) declared input**: not derived. The cell gives the kind (record, quote, replay placeholder, assumption, policy declaration, owner decision, structure declaration, evidence-class rule, check scope or allow-list entry) and its flag. A typed fact sentence names the record or decision behind it, or says "UNBACKED: typed narrative".
- **(c) presentation only**: wording, layout, CSS, chrome, routing or check mechanics that set no figure, label class, disposition or option order.
- **RESTATES <path>**: the item types a figure (or a fact) that a manifest or DERIVED path already holds. These are the defects the review is looking for.
- **UNBACKED**: a typed fact with no record or decision found.

**Status after the fragment.** After the builder's sweep, these flagged rows were fixed. Each now reads DERIVED or a manifest record:
- `loop-decisions.js:142`: "$194" is now Kirinyaga's excess kWh × electricity price;
- `:282`: "$6,430" and "30 Nov" are now the evaluated 6,500/no spend and the letter's lapse record;
- `:302`: "+0.6%" is now computed from the nodes;
- `:303`: "4,500 crate-days" is now MWEA-AGG's crates × days;
- `:334–335`: "one bus short" is now the evaluated buses short a night;
- `:48`: the 184 kWh battery estimate is now a numeric record (`usable_capacity_estimate_kwh`), no longer parsed from text;
- `loop-protect.js`: the penalties column is derived from the measure instead of the deleted typed `protect_effects.penalties`;
- Harvest idle crate-days now use the evaluated capacity;
- the audit's switch expectations now read derived-values.json.

**The remaining RESTATES rows** type a manifest record (a tariff, tolerance, event date, policy limit or replay window) in screen text. They are disposition (b), a record restated in code. They do not affect any evaluated figure or choice, but they can go stale if the record changes. The audit's hand-typed scan sees only literals equal to evaluated figures, so it cannot see them. This is **documented limit L-RS**.

**The remaining UNBACKED rows** are typed narrative facts (the obligation table, the verification texts, the "why first" rankings) with no record behind them. They are disposition (c) presentation, labelled as narrative, and are **documented limit L-UB**. Neither group holds a figure in place.

UNBACKED and RESTATES are counted on rows that also carry an (a), (b) or (c) disposition; one row can carry both. A row whose cell mixes dispositions is counted under its first one.

**Patterns worth fixing first.**

1. **Typed manifest records in screen code.** Tariff rates ($0.24, $0.12, $0.09, $0.15, $0.20, $0.40), tolerances (2%, 5%), the model's 24 °C and +2.5%, import limits (400 kW), Thika incident times and limits, and dates such as 30 Nov, 3 Oct, 6 Nov, 8 Nov and 17 Oct are typed in loop-core.js, loop-decisions.js, loop-portfolio.js and loop-site.js. `hand_typed_scan.py` only tests against DERIVED values, so it cannot catch any of these.
2. **Typed replay placeholders and assumptions.** "61 of 61 days", "35% at 5 am", "70%/90%", "80%, range 60–90%", "406 kW" and "6 / 10 more buses" are typed in many places. Some are used in screen arithmetic (`61 − n`).
3. **Typed DERIVED figures** that the scan misses because of its thresholds or units: "$6,430" (loop-decisions.js:282), "$194" (loop-decisions.js:142), "+0.6%" and "4,500 crate-days" (loop-decisions.js:302-303), "one bus short", and the switch expectations in audit.mjs:102 and 105.
4. **Stated manifest copies read where DERIVED holds the value**: `portfolio.chain`, `cost_per_departure_actual`, `sites_pct.Ruiru` (`ruiruDeliverable`), `period.variance`, `allocation.listed_not_summed`, the option `required_kwh`/`deliverable_kwh` and `buses_short`.
5. **Screen-held structure and decisions**: the `DEC` option sets and commits, the synthetic options with dispositions (KIS-OM-REVIEW, RUI-CONN-CLAIM), the `OBL` pass/fail table, the hard-coded Metro excluded list, `WHY_FIRST` ranking claims, `REPLAY_OPTS`, `DV_OPT`, `VNAME`/`PNODE` bindings, and the evidence-class overrides (`OBSERVER_MEASURED`, the chain-stage labels, the plan-row overrides).
6. **Defect**: loop-portfolio.js:204-207 shows a negative "Idle crate-days" for Harvest (`capc = null`).
7. **Stale figures** in the generated CHANGE-REGISTER.md and browser-audit.md (tools/gen_docs.cjs), for example "+$95,484 / $0", "+$2,556", "+$5,670" and "$6,096".

### tools/gen_docs.cjs

This generator writes CHANGE-REGISTER.md and browser-audit.md. Most of its text is hand-written history; figures typed into it are listed because those documents are figure-bearing (spec §7) and none of the typed figures carry `<!--fig:-->` tags. "Stale" means the typed value no longer equals DERIVED.

## 8. Items a perturbation would not reveal

A perturbation moves a figure only when the item is already binding. The items below can change a figure or a choice in some other world, but perturbing them in the five scanned worlds moves nothing, or they are not inputs at all. Each one is stated in the specification, so the independent model implements it separately, and the checker would catch any divergence between the two programs. A rule both programs share wrongly would not be caught. Those rules are listed here so a reviewer can judge each against the brief.

| # | Item | Where | Why a perturbation cannot show it | Disposition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| H1 | Tie-break order in the allocator (declared decision order, "none" first) | spec §6.3; E26 | no ties occur in this world | (b) structure declaration |
| H2 | Summing violations across decisions on their fallback (hours and money add) | spec §6.3; E25 | **reachable.** The final review's G20 (Mto genset 30 → 20 kW) puts Kisiwa and Mto on their fallbacks together, and 2,600 dollars of lost cover then add to hours. Not binding in the base world. | (b) structure declaration; **documented limit**: rank per bound, or normalise, before a world where it binds |
| H3 | Business days without holidays | spec §2.1; E4 | no holiday falls in the windows | (b); documented limit |
| H4 | Horizon months as whole years | E13 | the horizon is 12 months for every tenant | (b); documented limit |
| H5 | Rounding half-even on output only | spec §2.4; E3 | no figure sits on a half cent | (b) |
| H6 | `a \| b` fallbacks in `factor_map` (a missing record becomes the fallback, often 0) | spec §2.4; register §3.1 | a missing record is not a perturbation of a present value | (b) structure declaration. Each fallback is named in §3.1, and the C2 perturbation (rev 11) showed that removing Thika's unproven date gives 0 nights. A missing record is therefore not flagged as missing: **documented limit** |
| H7 | The guard `max(lost_unit_kw, 1)` in `shortfall_backed` | structures.MINI_GRID | only matters where no unit is lost (Kobo), where the result is 0 either way | (b) structure declaration |
| H8 | `months` default 1 for restore paths | spec §5.4 | it is part of the restore-path definition, not a value | (b) |
| H9 | The forward baseline expressions (`reduced_days: 0`, `uncovered_nights: 0`, `backup_unproven_days: 0`) | evaluation.forward_baseline | they reset leaves to 0 before options; perturbing 0 → 1 moves every forward option, and the scan does that (§3.1) | (b), listed in §3.1 |
| H10 | The claim states that count as notified | spec §4.1 | no right in this world has a notified claim with an open deadline | (b) |
| H11 | Which rights an option "touches" (cost line, restore, void, set, notice) | spec §4.1 | a new kind of touch would need a new field | (b) structure declaration |
| H12 | Case-key formatting (`True` / `False`) | spec §6.4 | text only | (c) |
| H13 | The checker's tolerance (0.001) and thresholds (money ≥ 50; O2 percentages below 100) | check_manifest.py | figures below the threshold are covered only where anchored | (b); documented limit |
| H14 | The influence scan's perturbation sizes (×1.1 + 1, +1 day) and its five worlds | scratchpad `rev13/influence.py` | a leaf that binds only beyond these sizes or in another world is listed as affecting nothing | **documented limit**; §3.2 lists those leaves individually so a reviewer can challenge any of them |

## 9. Documented limits

These are kept as limits, not fixed, and are disclosed here and in REVIEW-response.md:

- **H2.** Violations of different bounds add when two decisions are on their fallback at once.
- **H3.** There is no holiday calendar.
- **H4.** The horizon must be whole years.
- **H6.** A missing record falls back to its declared alternative. Narrowed in rev 16.13: each fallback now has a declared meaning (`empty_states.leaf_sources` `absent`), a number literal is read on an observed side only for a held-source zero or an option's identity value (C13), and on the target side only as the labelled target assumption, which `missing-records.json` names with the target line that would replace it.
- **H13.** Small figures (below 50) in companion documents are checked only where anchored.
- **H14.** The influence scan's sensitivity is bounded by its perturbation sizes and five worlds.
- **Typed text on screen.** The remaining typed figures on screen are the builder's allow-listed literals (§7), each with its reason. "406 kW" stays as a spike-E replay-placeholder basis.
- **L-RS.** Records restated in screen code (§7) are not checked against the record.
- **L-UB.** Typed narrative facts on screen (§7) have no record behind them and are labelled as narrative.
- **F3 (final review).** Rendered typed copies are unchecked. Numeric manifest leaves the screen renders directly are not compared with anything: `listed_not_summed`; MTO-BILLING charges; the huller's revenue and fuel; the `forward_replay` copies of the K and M parameters; `portfolio_constraints.budget_usd_nov_dec` beside `plans.*.budget`; the coverage shares; KAS-OEM-STOPGAP `nights`; site variances and bridge amounts, checked only against each other; the `excluded`, `recommended` and `switches[].cases` labels. They are correct today, and they can drift unseen.
- **F4.** The plan-facing option fields `value` and `valued_in_money` are not defined in the specification and are not compared by the checker (`NOT_COMPARED`), although the screens read them. Planting a wrong value there passes (EV1).
- **F6.** Typed narrative counts that restate evaluated figures, e.g. "the spare bus on one night" on the Kasarani page, §7 rows 170 and 175, and "6 more buses". The label L-RS ("records restated") understates them: these restate evaluated figures.
- **L1.** The influence scan covered 2,438 of 2,613 manifest leaves. The 175 left out include `oracle`, 32 leaves of illustration expectations that nothing enforces, which are (b) illustration expectations, unenforced. They also include `checks`, 8 statements of identities, some of which no code checks; these are (c) text.
- **L2.** H2 above: summing violations across bounds is reachable (G20).
- **L3.** Fallback eligibility is static: a decision falls back only when none of its options can meet the bound at all. A plan with crews 1 or budget 600 is therefore infeasible, although declared fallbacks exist. This is put to the owner as Q16.
- **L4.** The specification's authority over the independent model rests on review. The checker never reads the specification, so an edit to the specification alone passes (SP1). The specification-first order cannot be shown from the repository, because the files were committed together.
- **L5.** A wrong untagged figure that equals no key figure, outside main §2–§10, is not caught (Dr4); see also H13.
- **L6 (presentation).** "inverter inverter 2" on Act first (the huller condition), and "spare bus free on 0 night" under a changed roster. The evaluator docstring is corrected.
